                  (SourceCurrentAllocatedPartialState.advance caller
                    (advancePhysical caller.checked
                      {
                        heap :=
                          (SourcePruningEnumerationAction.prepare holeKey fits admits metadata nativeFuel
                              representativeFuel uv oldHandle scs oldValue caller.checked.native.machine.heap).state,
                        equality := caller.checked.operations })
                    caller.fourth caller.merge)
                  queries (some (action checked).run) (some attached)
                  (some
                    (SourcePruningRecordTypes.recordTypes graph templates callbackFuel metadata uv (Sum.inr newHandle)
                        attached.core).run)
                  none
        | x =>
          Result.narrowed checked
            (SourcePruningEnumerationAction.prepare holeKey fits admits metadata nativeFuel representativeFuel uv
              oldHandle scs oldValue caller.checked.native.machine.heap)
            (SourceCurrentAllocatedPartialState.advance caller
              (advancePhysical caller.checked
                {
                  heap :=
                    (SourcePruningEnumerationAction.prepare holeKey fits admits metadata nativeFuel representativeFuel
                        uv oldHandle scs oldValue caller.checked.native.machine.heap).state,
                  equality := caller.checked.operations })
              caller.fourth caller.merge)
            queries (some (action checked).run) none none none
  | x =>
    Result.stopped [Except.ok (UVarValue.unenumerated (some oldHandle) scs, readAfter)]
      (some (Except.error a✝ :: head✝ :: tail✝))

        | MergedSourceComparison.Result.done (_value, none) _heap =>

                        uv oldHandle scs oldValue caller.checked.native.machine.heap).state,
                  equality := caller.checked.operations })
              caller.fourth caller.merge)
            queries none none none none
        | MergedSourceComparison.Result.done (value, some action) heap =>
          match (action checked).run with
          | [Except.ok (PUnit.unit, observed)] =>
            match encodeNode value with
            | none =>
              Result.narrowed checked
                (SourcePruningEnumerationAction.prepare holeKey fits admits metadata nativeFuel representativeFuel uv

                      equality := caller.checked.operations })
                  caller.fourth caller.merge)
                queries (some (action checked).run) none none none

              match
                SourcePruningEnumerationNarrowAdapter.adapt heap indexFuel uv oldHandle newHandle scs before
                  observed with
              | none =>

                        equality := caller.checked.operations })

                          equality := caller.checked.operations })

                          attached.core).run)

                        (SourceCurrentAllocatedPartialState.advance caller

                                (SourcePruningEnumerationAction.prepare holeKey fits admits metadata nativeFuel
